# Ghatana Media — remaining feature implementation and owner decisions

**Current owner readback:** [Engineering decisions](ENGINEERING-DECISIONS.md) separate the legacy upload model from the actual runtime enum, align the runtime stream type, authorize independent task starts, and preserve history pending qualified archival. [The structured register](remaining-work.json) reconciles **30 VERIFIED / 72 OPEN / 1 OPTIONAL DEFERRED** (103 total). The remaining open units comprise 31 independently startable engineering tasks, 10 safe engineering tasks requiring real-positive inputs later, 30 journey scenario/test tasks and one archive/delete task. `requires` identifies full integration prerequisites, not a blanket pause on code and tests.

> **Current engineering selector.** This file and [machine-readable remaining work](remaining-work.json) replace dated task-run selections **for feature implementation only**. Product definitions retain semantic authority. No gtool setup, closure, evidence, convergence, release or production cutover work is authorized by this document.

## Current source and safe readiness claims

- Inspected `main@5cc47191` (November 5, 2026): the four reported commits are present; 56 intent-scoped docs exist; orphaned test-only modules were removed. The two new durable file stores are **single-host adapters**, not admission of real agent tools or Data Cloud finality.
- Safety correction `c38620ff`: exclusive link claim rather than misleading `ATOMIC_MOVE` no-replace, removed unsafe legacy upsert, persistent pre-dispatch uncertainty and correct hydration error handling, added contention/crash-phase/mismatched-identity tests. Focused Gradle selectors for migration lineage, provider/job crash recovery, multimedia restart, spatial export, and audio/video capture passed in this review; this is not a full repository build or a production cutover.
- The source-state denominators (28 physical / 21 logical / 7 compositions; 30 journeys; 47 screens; 30 HTTP + 44 gRPC named operations) are not counts of unimplemented features. This register has 103 task units, including 30 compositional outcome tasks. Its units overlap original historic F/V/W3/quality tasks and must never be added as independent obligations.

## Binding owner decisions (implementation approval, not fabricated external credentials)

1. **CORE_RUNTIME_INDEPENDENT_OF_DATA_CLOUD.** Media implements and persists its own artifact/job/rights/stream/transcript/caption state. Data Cloud is an optional, downstream index/metadata/run-ledger projection only. The existing `MediaProcessorPort` is not an authorized processing API: it accepts artifactId, tenantId and string parameters but no trusted actor, immutable version, current source purpose or rights readback. Keep its three adapters unavailable; do not wire it as a second execution route. Absence of Data Cloud cannot block Media runtime, model work, UI or J01–J30. Positive Data Cloud publication tests are optional integration work, not a core acceptance requirement.
2. **SINGLE_OCR_GATEWAY.** Document Intelligence owns generic OCR/parser/engine selection and its public API. Media may sample frames, authorize ROI/source versions/PTS and consume DI; never add a Media OCR engine or depend on Data Cloud for OCR.
3. **DURABLE_EFFECT_FIRST.** No real Agent Tool action or external ledger write until exclusive claim, source authorization, pre-effect durable UNKNOWN and authentic post-effect reconciliation are working. File stores are single-host; multi-process must use a genuine shared CAS or reject concurrent writers. Replayed terminal results require fresh caller authorization, not merely matching envelope metadata.
4. **SDK_NO_FICTIONAL_ROUTES.** The 33-row legacy SDK registry includes three parser artifacts (`for`, `if`, `clearTimeout`); 60 source declarations are not 60 approved callable operations. Require an AST-derived per-symbol classification before any add/remove/rename. Preserve public ESM/CJS ABI. `MPSEM-0023-C002` is a stale observation about a legacy multimodal path: do not invent or enable `/api/v1/media/multimodal-analyses`. Reconcile as explicitly non-dispatched compatibility while real multimodal work proceeds via the currently admitted canonical operation/route. Update source parity and tests after verifying the actual dispatch branch.
5. **PROVIDER_RIGHTS_ARE_REAL_INPUTS.** Engineering can implement all paths/tests now, use safe synthetic fixtures for negative/fault tests and typed `NOT_ADMITTED` on missing real providers. Positive licensed inference requires exact artifact/license/export lineage, rights, voice/biometric authorization and actual reference data. Choose official source-pinned Whisper candidate and English/Nepali Common Voice v27 benchmark pathway if individually verified; Hindi/FLEURS remain deferred for this selected profile. A lawful alternative provider through the same public SPI is allowed. No self-issued vendor license or qualified corpus.
6. **CREATIVE_ENGINES_ARE_PRODUCT_WORK.** Timeline, renderer, 2D/3D, solver, sound, dubbing, review, export and Player are not covered by an effect-free compiler, UI preview or `NOT_ADMITTED` response. Implement actual usable outcomes and tests, reusing existing ecosystem engines first.
7. **DOC_CLEANUP_NONBLOCKING.** The approximately 995 MB under `docs/implementation/` must be classified by hash, refs and retention before deletion. Allow routine code work concurrently. Approve migration out of active tree for redundant dated logs/snapshots once stable reference redirects, required immutable preservation and regression checks have been completed; do not erase protected source preimages based on filename alone.
8. **ENGINEERING_AUTHORIZATION.** All local source changes, refactors, test fixtures, public-interface adapters, nonproduction simulations and documentation consolidation within the accepted product meaning are authorized without further owner prompts. Never represent this as legal model/voice consent, external-key authority, independent acceptance, deployment approval or release qualification.

## Exact reusable implementation method

Every task has one source owner and no second source of truth. Follow its `where`, `what`, `how`, `acceptance` and `requires` fields in the JSON register. Before editing, diff current code/tests against the task to avoid repeating implemented behavior. Develop with failure injection and real positive provider effects when the prerequisite is genuinely available. Run focused tests, then affected source-free consumers; keep truthful `NOT_ADMITTED` on missing external inputs rather than inventing success. Keep per-surface docs under `docs/surfaces/` current. One task can complete independently without waiting on unrelated surfaces.

**`media.dev.transactional-event-outbox`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `modules/infrastructure/messaging`, `providers/aws-postgresql`, `launcher`
- **What:** Publish lifecycle events transactionally without conflating delivery ACK with job finality.
- **How:** Use existing outbox identity with claim leases, destination status and backoff; separate appended/delivered/consumed/effected.
- **Test/exit:** Lost ACK, double consumer, partition, crash-after-publish and readback parity tests.
- **Progress:** 18/18 focused provider, relay and consumer tests pass. This pulse, `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSourceTest` passes 2/2 after replacing a sleep-based lost-ACK race with direct DB claim expiry after broker acceptance; same event ID is redelivered and the stale worker cannot acknowledge the replaced claim. Local append rollback, claim/fencing, retry, lost-ACK, partition, dedupe and readback checks are covered. Current source confirms runtime ServiceLoader drains V016 `media_processing_lifecycle_outbox`, while job acceptance/finality still append to V011 `media_lifecycle_outbox`; source does not say V011 is retired or specify stable event identity/version remapping and drain proof. Preserve both families without routing or suppressing events until the owner resolves that exact compatibility decision. Event Plane append/lookup admission also lacks a durable receipt. 2026-11-06 increment: PostgreSQL outbox claim now uses exact arithmetic for the signed BIGINT fencing generation; at Long.MAX_VALUE overflow rolls back the claim before provider publish. The adverse H2 regression confirms zero publisher calls and unchanged token/owner/published state. `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSourceTest` PASS 2/2. V011/V016 event-family mapping and the Event Plane durable append receipt remain owner/platform blockers; no rows were remapped or suppressed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `MediaLifecycleEventConsumerTest` + `MediaLifecycleOutboxRelayTest` PASS 13/13. A projection that commits before its response is lost returns `CONSUMPTION_UNKNOWN`; replay returns `DUPLICATE` with `NON_FINAL_APPEND` and terminal-readback-required, while materialization remains one. V011/V016 family mapping and durable Event Plane receipts remain owner/platform blockers.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `VideoFrameReconciler.resume` accepts the highest valid acknowledged frame sequence while preserving the reserved exhausted cursor; larger ACKs remain rejected. The regression confirms re-submitting the terminal sequence is `DUPLICATE` and does not move the cursor. `GuardedVideoStreamSessionTest` passes 19/19. Source artifact/version/PTS/timebase binding, authenticated transport and qualified source-clock evidence remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `MediaWorkflowMetrics.metricIdentity` rejects recognizable AWS temporary key (`ASIA…`), Google OAuth token (`ya29.…`) and GitLab token (`glpat-…`) prefixes before metric labels are registered. Regressions cover profile and quality-series construction plus absence from registry tags; `MediaWorkflowMetricsTest` passes 21/21. Canonical render producers, deployed scrape/alert delivery, real 1/16/64 load/quota qualification and provider admission remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** An H2 regression sets V011 `publication_attempts` to `Integer.MAX_VALUE` and proves the checked claim abort leaves the event unpublished, claim owner/token/expiry null, counter unchanged, and no delivery-attempt row. `PostgresqlMediaOutboxPublicationSourceTest` passes 8/8 with zero skips. This is local transaction evidence only; V011/V016 event-family mapping/drain ownership and durable Event Plane append receipts remain unresolved.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** V016 `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSource.claim` now rejects an exhausted persisted attempt counter before publisher dispatch. Its H2 regression verifies zero publisher calls and unchanged attempt, claim, and published state; `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSourceTest` passes both test methods. V011/V016 family mapping/drain ownership and Event Plane durable append receipts remain unresolved.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Lifecycle outbox ACK and retry/dead-letter updates now require the persisted claim lease to still be live as well as owner/token match, preventing an expired worker from changing ACK or retry state before a replacement claim. The H2 concurrency/expiry selector passes 2 JUnit tests (including an expired-owner ACK regression). Event Plane append receipts and V011/V016 family mapping/drain ownership remain open; no job-finality or cutover claim.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** 2026-11-06 expired-release increment: `releaseClaim` now records an expired still-claimed delivery attempt as `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN/CLAIM_EXPIRED_WITHOUT_ACK`; only a live lease becomes `CLAIM_RELEASED`. The H2 regression verifies the event stays unpublished and the next claim retains attempt history `[OUTCOME_UNKNOWN, CLAIMED]`. `PostgresqlMediaOutboxPublicationSourceTest` PASS 9/9, no skips. V011/V016 event-family mapping and durable Event Plane append receipts remain unresolved; no cutover or job-finality claim.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Retry deadline calculation now uses checked epoch-millisecond addition and rejects nonpositive delays, preventing signed overflow from making a retry immediately eligible. Boundary regressions cover normal, zero, and overflowing deadlines; `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSourceTest` PASS 3/3. No event family was remapped or published. V011/V016 mapping/drain ownership and durable Event Plane append receipts remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** PostgreSQL lifecycle outbox now rejects positive `claimLease` durations that truncate below 1 ms, preventing an immediately expired persisted claim and duplicate concurrent publish. The regression and full `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSourceTest` pass 4/4 with no skips. V011/V016 event-family and drain ownership plus durable Event Plane append receipts remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** Before V016 lifecycle publication, the decoded payload `eventId` must exactly match the claimed row `event_id`; mismatch follows the existing failure path and leaves the row unpublished. The H2 regression confirms zero publisher calls, one recorded attempt, and no published timestamp; `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSourceTest` passes 4/4 with no skips. V011/V016 event-family/drain ownership and durable Event Plane append receipts remain unresolved; no event was remapped or suppressed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** V016 publication now validates the claimed row’s tenant, aggregate identity/version, event type and classification against the decoded event, along with event ID, before dispatch. A tenant-row tamper regression records one failure attempt, zero publisher calls and no published timestamp; `PostgresqlMediaLifecycleOutboxSourceTest` passes 4/4. V011/V016 family mapping/drain ownership and durable Event Plane append receipts remain unresolved.
- **Coordinator module review (2026-11-07):** Current FULL source-composite `MediaLifecycleEventConsumerTest` and `MediaLifecycleOutboxRelayTest` pass 17/17. Existing messaging code covers payload/envelope binding, tenant/correlation/aggregate parity, authorization before projection, fingerprint-aware dedupe/conflict, typed UNKNOWN and terminal readback; relay coverage includes ACK-loss redelivery and expired-lease no-publish. No source-defined local gap was found. Keep V011/V016 family mapping/drain ownership and durable Event Plane append/lookup receipt as owner/platform blockers; no event family was remapped or suppressed.
- **Predecessors:** `media.dev.canonical-job-reconciliation`

**`media.dev.rights-aware-cache`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `modules/infrastructure/cache`, `launcher`
- **What:** Ensure caches cannot outlive source version, tenant rights or revocation.
- **How:** Include immutable source/tenant/policy epoch in cache key; invalidate on retention/consent/right changes; fail closed on stale authority.
- **Test/exit:** Cross-tenant, revocation, restart, eviction, stale metadata, stampede and outage tests.
- **Progress:** Full cache unit selector passes 104 tests and Redis/Testcontainers integration selector passes 11 tests (115 total; no failures or skips), including typed scoped reads after Redis client restart. The scoped API uses typed `CURRENT`/`REJECTED`/`UNKNOWN` owner-authority readback with exact `CacheKeyScope` equality; it rechecks before cache lookup and disclosure, fails closed on mismatch/error/revocation, and best-effort evicts only the exact scoped key. Legacy generic Boolean cache access fails closed without cache reads/writes or loader calls; the transcription service's legacy Boolean overload is source-read-only and never reads/writes protected cache entries. Tests include stale policy epoch, unknown/rejected/error outcomes, revocation between lookup and disclosure, exact-key eviction, Redis client restart, and legacy paths. Test callbacks are fixtures, not proof of owner authority. Coordinator review 2026-11-06: the current public lifecycle event contract has no rights/consent/retention/policy-epoch/cache-invalidation events, and its redacted aggregate IDs cannot identify a prior cache scope. No safe event-driven mapping is available. Still OPEN: no trusted current source/rights provider or Media runtime caller is wired, event-driven invalidation needs an owner-defined invalidation identity/epoch plus replay/readback contract, and the transcription service remains source-read-through; do not claim active runtime caching. 2026-11-06 increment: Scoped authority readback now resolves false after the existing cache operation timeout, so a never-completing read cannot hold the caller indefinitely or expose cached data. `AudioVideoCacheKeyRedactionTest` PASS 13/13; the regression verifies no cache get, put, or loader call on timeout. Test authority is synthetic; owner-backed runtime authority wiring remains open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Generic raw `get`, `put` and `getOrLoad` now reject protected `av2p-` and legacy shared `av2-` keys before cache access; new unprotected keys use `av2u-`, and scoped cache operations use the internal authority-checked path. Added a raw-key bypass regression and changed cross-scope/source-fingerprint tests to use typed current-scope authority. Full cache unit and Redis integration suites passed, including restart/revocation/tenant-isolation cases. This is synthetic-authority test evidence only. Task remains OPEN pending owner-backed rights/runtime wiring and an owner-defined replayable invalidation identity/contract; no active runtime cache is claimed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** The focused protected-key bypass and revocation selectors passed 2/2; the full `:services:media:modules:infrastructure:cache:test` suite passed, and `:services:media:modules:infrastructure:cache:integrationTest` passed 11 Redis cases. All were run with `-Pghatana.media.dependencyProfile=DEV_SOURCE_COMPOSITE -Pghatana.media.compositeScope=FULL --no-daemon --no-parallel --max-workers=2 --console=plain`. The task remains OPEN pending owner-backed rights/runtime wiring and an owner-defined replayable invalidation identity/contract.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Public raw `invalidate(key)` now rejects protected `av2p-` and legacy `av2-` keys before cache-port access; scoped invalidation uses the authority-checked internal path. `AudioVideoCacheTest` passes 41/41, including zero raw invalidation effects and successful exact scoped invalidation. Owner-backed rights/runtime wiring and replayable invalidation identity remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** An authority readback marked `CURRENT` for another tenant/principal/rights scope now has a focused regression proving no cache lookup, source-loader call or payload disclosure occurs; cleanup invalidates only the requested scope key. `AudioVideoCacheTest.currentAuthorityForAnotherScopeCannotAuthorizeLookupOrDisclosure` passes 1/1 with zero skips. Test authority only; trusted owner/runtime wiring and replayable invalidation identity remain undefined, and no active runtime cache is claimed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Added protected-key regressions proving source artifact/transcript version changes use a distinct cache key and tenant/rights-policy scope changes cannot reuse the prior key. AudioVideoCacheTest PASS 44/44 under FULL source composite. Synthetic key-scope evidence only; owner-backed runtime rights wiring and replayable invalidation identity remain open; no active runtime cache is claimed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** scope-canonicalization increment: `CacheKeyScope` now rejects surrounding whitespace on all ten protected identity/version/policy scope components. The field-by-field adversarial regression in `AudioVideoCacheKeyRedactionTest` and the full cache module unit selector pass under `DEV_SOURCE_COMPOSITE/FULL`. This is local canonicalization evidence only; no owner-backed authority wiring, replayable rights-invalidation contract, or integrated runtime cache is claimed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** `CacheKeyScope` rejects Unicode whitespace/space separators at both ends of all ten protected components, including NBSP U+00A0. Prefix/suffix adversarial coverage and the full cache module unit task pass (111 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips). Redis integration was not rerun. Owner-backed rights/runtime wiring and replayable invalidation identity remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** model-license scope: `CacheKeyScope` now requires the source-defined model-license reference and includes it in both protected cache-key builders. **`media.dev.stream-session-authority`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `modules/audio-streaming`, `modules/video-streaming`, `launcher`
- **What:** Complete canonical stream frame ordering, consent, lease, source clock and recovery.
- **How:** Bind both streams to one canonical session and durable ACK cursor; keep module helpers as non-authoritative implementation details.
- **Test/exit:** Gap/reorder/lease expiry/revoke while live, backpressure, EOS, abort, restart and 1/16/64 clients.

Added helper-only in-process 1/16/64 guarded-session tiers and require exact durable session revision for video resume; module tests cover gap/reorder, revoke, bounded backpressure, detach/cancel, EOS/lost ACK and local resume. The canonical audio frame adapter rejects video-kind sessions and returns ACK only after provider sequence matches and a fresh canonical store readback confirms session identity, generation, exact post-CAS version, acknowledged sequence, buffer count, cleared in-flight reservation, finality and resulting state; missing readback returns `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` and blocks blind retry. `MediaRuntimeCanonicalStreamFramePortTest` passes 8/8. New focused persistence checks pass: `PostgresqlMediaStreamReconnectExpiryTest` 2/2 rejects reconnect after persisted lease expiry, including a row-lock delay crossing expiry and a frozen runtime clock; `MediaRuntimePostgresqlAudioStreamRestartTest` 2/2 uses separate worker JVMs to read durable audio/video ACK cursors after restart and prevents replay of acknowledged frames. These do not bind source coordinates: canonical frame state still has no immutable source artifact/version or source PTS/timebase. Still OPEN: source cursor/version/PTS binding, authenticated transport and real source-clock provenance; 1/16/64 results remain helper-only. 2026-11-06 source disposition: PDP-1 session intent names exact source version/clock, but the current canonical frame wire carries tenant/session/sequence and payload/EOS only; durable ACK rows store sequence/revision/finality without source artifact/version or PTS/timebase. The stream-session operation still leaves frame-level source binding unresolved. Do not add fields or persist guessed provenance. Focused durable lease-expiry and cross-process restart selectors passed 4 tests. Task remains OPEN pending owner definition of frame-level source cursor/PTS/timebase linkage, authenticated transport, and qualified source-clock evidence. 2026-11-06 increment: `GuardedAudioStreamSession` now consumes a delivered candidate before invoking the provider sink; a sink exception produces a bounded `ProviderOutcomeUnknownException` without preserving provider exception details, and a retry cannot invoke the sink again. `GuardedAudioStreamSessionTest` passes 17/17. This is a helper-level in-memory retry fence only; canonical durable source cursor/version/PTS binding, authenticated transport and qualified source-clock evidence remain open.

Each changed-clock candidate returns `UNKNOWN` before provider offer; the acknowledged prefix and cursor remain unchanged. No source fields were added to the wire. Durable source cursor/version/PTS binding, authenticated transport and qualified source-clock evidence remain open.

This is helper-level in-memory evidence; canonical durable source cursor/version/PTS binding, authenticated transport and qualified source-clock evidence remain open.

A committed frame hidden from readback returns OUTCOME_UNKNOWN; retry with the stale binding is NOT_ADMITTED and does not redispatch. Focused `MediaRuntimeCanonicalVideoStreamFramePortTest` PASS 8/8. This establishes local ACK/readback behavior only; source artifact/version/PTS/timebase binding, authenticated transport, and qualified source-clock provenance remain owner/platform blockers.

Corrected a contradictory sequence-boundary assertion: MAX_FRAME_SEQUENCE is a valid durable ACK and resumes to the exhausted Integer.MAX_VALUE cursor; no further frame is accepted. VideoFrameReconcilerTest PASS 16/16. This remains in-memory helper evidence; canonical durable source cursor/version/PTS binding, authenticated transport, consent integration, and qualified source-clock evidence remain open.

**`media.dev.http-operation-parity`** · OPEN

OpenAPI no longer asserts replay safety or payload-hash scopes the handlers/stores do not implement. Still OPEN: consent grant lacks the owner-defined requestId/delegation/authority-receipt HTTP binding; stream open lacks a durable replay receipt for its one-time connection token. Do not automatically retry either mutation while owner mapping and durable receipt semantics remain NOT_ADMITTED/proposal-only. 2026-11-06 full route audit: inspected all 30 manifest routes; OpenAPI, runtime registry, and manifest inventories match. Current handlers and source-free tests cover route dispatch/auth across health, consent, uploads (restart/exact/conflicting replay/readback), jobs (including typed UNKNOWN), streams (identity isolation and 202 headers), providers, and captions. No well-defined missing handler was found. Still OPEN: consent grant has no owner requestId/delegation/authority-receipt mapping; stream open has no durable replay receipt for its one-time token, so retries remain unsupported. 2026-11-06 coordinator source disposition: the PDP-3 consent and stream protocol-operation observations both have `logicalOperationRef: null`, unresolved binding, and owner review pending. PDP-1 `consent-record-grant.v1` requires trusted delegation, `requestId`, `authorityRef`, and `observedAt`, while its `sourceAdapterBoundary` says the current OpenAPI request/response lacks those fields and is `NOT_ADMITTED`. PDP-1 `media-stream-session-open` separately defines request identity, canonical payload fingerprint, receipt, and same-key reconciliation, but the observed HTTP route has no `requestId` or durable one-time-token replay receipt. Keep both HTTP mutations single-shot, do not retry automatically, and do not widen either wire schema or infer equivalence. Owner field-level HTTP binding and stream-token receipt semantics remain the exact missing inputs.

Durable relay-to-public projection and authoritative terminal readback integration remain unproved.

- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** `MediaLifecycleEventConsumer` fingerprints every parsed v1 payload field using canonical key order and type/value length-prefix encoding, passing the fingerprint to `ProjectionStore`. Same event ID with the same fingerprint remains a duplicate; a changed fingerprint returns `CONFLICTING_REPLAY` before projection. `ProjectionStore` retains its 5-argument abstract method and adds a fail-closed fingerprint-aware default; legacy stores return typed `UNAVAILABLE` with no effect. A new test proves a 5-argument lambda still compiles and is not called. Public v1 ABI is unchanged; `MediaLifecycleEventConsumerTest` passes 13/13. Still OPEN: active Shared relay payload/schema mismatch, missing `principalId`, unreconciled V011/V016 outbox families, and no durable public projection or authoritative terminal readback integration.

Shared source at local commit `314db14fc` exposes `subjectId` but no consumer `principalId` or subject-to-principal mapping, so identity derivation remains an owner handoff. V011/V016 reconciliation and durable projection/readback remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** `MediaPublicLifecycleEvent.fromPayload` now accepts JSON integer `aggregateVersion` values represented by `BigInteger` when they fit the existing positive `long` contract, and rejects overflow without truncation. `MediaPublicLifecycleEventTest` passes 7/7, including `Long.MAX_VALUE` acceptance and overflow rejection. The Multimodal tests prove that a valid test identity reaches source authorization with its exact tenant/principal and malformed principal identity is rejected before authorization or provider dispatch. These tests use local fixtures and do not establish production trust or an identical principal-format policy across services. On 2026-11-06, ONNX Whisper streaming close was hardened to clear buffered audio, accumulated transcript and callbacks; synchronized feed/end/close and callback registration prevent close races and callback retention after close. `WhisperStreamingLifecycleTest` passes. Equivalent cleanup was added to the Whisper.cpp session, but it was not loaded or executed because the adapter's static initializer requires `libwhisper`; this remains source-only evidence. Broader auth parity, native leak auditing and real provider/host qualification remain open. 2026-11-06 increment: added a source-free WhisperCppStreamingLifecycleTest that initializes only the static nested session (not the native-loading outer adapter) and proves close is idempotent, buffered audio and callback references are released, and late callback registration is ignored. Focused selector PASS 1/1. The native adapter/model was not loaded; no inference, host or provider qualification is implied. Broader auth parity, native leak audit, and real provider qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `RateLimitingServerInterceptor` now releases its concurrency permit when handler startup throws and guards release to one call across repeated close/close-then-throw. `RateLimitingServerInterceptorTest` passes 5/5. This is local interceptor lifecycle evidence only; broader auth parity, native leak audit and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `PiperOnnxEngine.close()` now attempts environment and executor cleanup even if closing the session throws. Focused lifecycle regressions pass 2/2, including propagation of a session-close `Error` after cleanup; no ONNX runtime/model was loaded. Broader auth parity, native leak-rate auditing and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Piper inference input tensors are now constructed inside cleanup scope and every input close is attempted; cleanup failures are suppressed without replacing the primary inference failure. `PiperOnnxEngineLifecycleTest` passes 3/3. This is local lifecycle evidence only; no ONNX model was loaded, and native leak-rate, licensed model/voice and platform qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `ErrorHandler.executeWithFallback` now propagates both JDK and Shared `CancellationException` types without invoking fallback. `ErrorHandlerTest` passes 15/15, including identity and zero-fallback assertions for both types. Broader auth parity, native leak audit and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `libs/common` RetryPolicy now propagates JDK and Shared CancellationException unchanged even when a broad retry predicate accepts all failures; focused RetryPolicyTest PASS 25/25. This is local cancellation behavior only; broader auth parity, native leak auditing, and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** 2026-11-06 retry-parity increment: `StreamingRetryHandler` now propagates Shared platform `CancellationException` unchanged, matching `libs/common` RetryPolicy and preventing broad retry predicates or fallback from consuming cancellation. Focused `StreamingRetryHandlerTest` PASS 15/15. This is retry behavior parity only; remaining pool/security/adapter consolidation is still open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `ErrorHandler.executeWithHandling` now propagates both JDK and Shared `CancellationException` unchanged instead of translating/logging cancellation as an internal failure. `ErrorHandlerTest` PASS 16/16 with identity assertions. Broader auth parity, native leak auditing, and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `InputValidationServerInterceptor` latches an oversized-stream rejection: after the first over-limit protobuf, later messages are not forwarded and the call closes only once. The regression selector passes 3/3. Broader service auth parity, native leak auditing and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** 2026-11-06 retry-classification increment: `ErrorHandler.isRetryable` now treats JDK and Shared cancellation exceptions as terminal before message/cause heuristics, even when their messages or nested causes look transient. `ErrorHandlerTest` PASS 17/17 under FULL source composite. This is local cancellation parity only; broader auth parity, native leak auditing, and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `ErrorHandler.isRetryable` now tracks cause objects by identity and terminates safely on a cyclic cause chain, without changing the public ABI. The cyclic-cause regression and focused `ErrorHandlerTest` pass 18/18; multimodal consumer interop selector passes 2 tests. Broader authentication parity, native-resource leak audit, and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** `ContextAwareBoundedExecutor.Submission.result()` now routes direct future cancellation through the submission's terminal fence. Started effect-capable work preserves `UNKNOWN_OUTCOME`; cancellation honors `mayInterruptIfRunning`, and returns false if normal completion already won. `RpcDeadlineServerInterceptorTest` passes 20/20, including direct cancellation, completion-race, and noninterrupting cancellation cases. This is local executor semantics only; broader auth parity, native leak audits and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** Configured WhisperCpp, Whisper ONNX, and YOLO adapters reject model switching to IDs they cannot load; Piper rejects unconfigured voices instead of changing active-voice state. Blank/null IDs are invalid, and rejected IDs are not copied into errors. Four focused tests and common-module compilation pass under `DEV_SOURCE_COMPOSITE`; no native/model inference was loaded. Broader auth parity, native leak auditing, and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** native lifecycle increment: a fair read/write lease now fences `WhisperCppAdapter.transcribe` and `warmup` from `close`; close waits for active native use, disposes once, rejects later work and always shuts down the async executor. Native-library-free `NativeModelLifecycleTest` PASS 2/2 plus `WhisperCppStreamingLifecycleTest` PASS 1/1. No native inference/free ABI was exercised; authentication parity, native leak audit and real provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** `RateLimitingServerInterceptor` now releases a held concurrency permit when either the gRPC listener or its `Context` is cancelled, idempotently with normal close/startup-failure cleanup. Limit-one regressions admit a second call after both cancellation paths; `RateLimitingServerInterceptorTest` passes 7/7. This is local interceptor lifecycle evidence only; broader auth parity, native leak audits and provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** HealthMetricsServer.start now rejects a repeated active start before replacing the live HttpServer/executor handles; start/close are synchronized to prevent a lifecycle race. HealthMetricsServerTest.repeatedStartDoesNotLoseTheLiveServerHandle confirms the original /health endpoint remains available. Focused common-module selector PASS 5/5. Local lifecycle evidence only; broader service auth parity, native leak audit and provider/host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** `EnginePool` now destroys or retains a pool-owned engine for explicit cleanup retry when health checking throws a fatal `Error` during borrow or return, then propagates the original error. `EnginePoolLifecycleTest` passes under the FULL source composite with borrow/return regressions. The legacy `SttStreamingLatencyEnhancer` increments `batchesEmitted` only after its emitter returns; its throwing-emitter test confirms failed delivery is not counted and bytes are released (`SttStreamingLatencyEnhancerTest` 15/15). `MediaCaptionRuntime` selects only one ready transcript parent store and ignores development-only stores in production-like profiles (`MediaCaptionRuntimeTest` 6/6). This prevents a mixed-provenance aggregate but does not establish source-bound durable child identity, calibrated quality, production clock mapping, governed rights or real qualified child outputs.

This is local structural validation only; timestamps remain unqualified, confidence remains heuristic/uncalibrated, and durable child identity, rights integration, and real qualified outputs remain open.

This is synthetic local provenance only; real child outputs, source/clock/rights bindings, durable child identity, and calibration remain open.

No calibration or live provider qualification is claimed.

Synthetic temporal-association evidence only; source/clock lineage, real child outputs, rights and calibrated quality remain open.

A stale restore after undo/redo now also proves that CAS reports the redo revision and preserves the current head; focused `ProjectTimelineEngineTest` passes 10/10. Still OPEN: canonical durable store/launcher binding is unavailable; production persistence remains typed NOT_ADMITTED and UI bindings must not become another source of truth.

For the executable request boundary, the coordinator follows the more specific owner wire schema: 1–128 ASCII characters, leading alphanumeric, then alphanumeric/dot/underscore/tilde/colon/hyphen. Engine, in-memory, PostgreSQL and NOT_ADMITTED store boundaries validate that rule; tests include colon acceptance and invalid first character/non-ASCII/overlength. Timeline selector passes 11; PostgreSQL store selector passes 9 with Testcontainers. Source prose/schema reconciliation remains an owner handoff. No PDP source or formal status changed; project graph mapping and production wiring remain unresolved. 2026-11-06 coordinator source disposition: PDP-1 create-project requires its initial `ProjectRevision` to contain zero timelines; the local `Graph.empty(timelineId)` always carries a timeline identity, and no owner mapping says that identity is only an editor handle rather than a `media.domain.timeline`. Keep the graph and V030 store as isolated development/test adapters and do not wire them as canonical project revisions until the owner defines that mapping.

A create request ID rebound to a different title now has explicit test coverage for typed `IdempotencyConflictException`; an exact replay still returns the original project and initial revision with unchanged head. This follows the owner wire schema binding `requestId` to exact workspace and title. Rust comparison integrity now provides borrowing accessors and an `undo()` copy; the integration test proves mutation of that copy cannot alter the stored comparison. `cargo test --offline` passes 68 unit plus 1 integration test, with formatting and clippy clean. A 2026-11-06 regression fixed the noise gate treating any nonzero threshold below `f64::EPSILON` as silence; valid sub-epsilon audio now receives attenuation while the input remains unchanged. Public fields remain source-compatible, so callers should treat comparison values as snapshots. Synthetic checks do not qualify perceptual quality. The local noise-profile gate and linear declipper lack lawful representative-corpus quality qualification; no learned model/provider or durable project-timeline A/B wiring is admitted. 2026-11-06 increment: Rust declipper stereo regression proves reconstruction changes only the clipped channel, leaves the other channel unchanged, and preserves source bytes. Offline Cargo suite PASS 69 unit + 1 integration + doc tests; `cargo fmt --check` and `git diff --check` pass. Synthetic fixture is not perceptual quality evidence; lawful corpus qualification, independent quality reference, and durable timeline A/B remain OPEN. 2026-11-06 increment: `stereoCorrelation` now returns no stereo statistic unless the PCM layout has exactly two channels; the 3-channel regression prevents channels 0/1 from being mislabeled as a stereo measurement. `AudioRepairComparisonTest` PASS 8/8. - **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** PcmStemMixer bounds quantized PCM samples to the requested ceiling and fails closed to silence when the estimated inter-sample peak still exceeds very low caps, reporting zero applied gain/metrics consistently. Numeric boundary evidence only; lawful source, certified true-peak/perceptual qualification, durable project revision, and production mastering remain open.

**`media.dev.multitrack-composition-render`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `modules/production/rendering`, `libs/audio-video-ui`
- **What:** Render combined clips, narration, captions, graphics, audio and color accurately.

- **Progress:** Added a bounded deterministic media-graph planner with exact rational VFR mapping, caption/transition validation, immutable source refs, graph/output limits and canonical manifest digest. Input list lengths are checked before copying, and iterator consumption is capped even if a custom `List` reports an inaccurate size. Added internal `BoundedRenderOutput` with cumulative byte-budget rejection before overflow reaches its delegate and a digest only after clean close; `MultitrackRenderPlannerTest` passes 10/10 for exact cap, overflow, partial write/close failures and cleanup. The sink is not wired to a codec or runtime, so actual output-space enforcement remains unclaimed. Codec execution remains typed `NOT_ADMITTED`/`UNAVAILABLE`; no playable mux, audio/visual/caption sync, cancellation or partial-result recovery is claimed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Caption values now reject unpaired UTF-16 surrogates before canonical UTF-8 manifest encoding, preventing malformed strings from collapsing to the same replacement-character digest. Regression coverage includes lone high/low and split surrogate sequences plus valid supplementary Unicode. The full rendering module test task passes. This is local manifest-integrity evidence; codec execution, playable mux, and synchronization remain unclaimed.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `BoundedRenderOutput` now remains failed when a delegate throws an `Error` after partially writing, including during write, flush, or close; cleanup cannot expose a completion digest for partial output. `MultitrackRenderPlannerTest` passes 12/12. No codec/runtime effect is enabled; playable mux, synchronization, cancellation/readback, rights, and project-revision integration remain unavailable.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** MultitrackRenderPlanner now rejects whitespace-padded clip/source artifact/version identity values instead of trimming them before manifest hashing. No codec execution or playable render is claimed; runtime output, codec, cancellation/readback, rights, and project-revision integration remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Public multitrack `Plan` construction now bounds defensive copying of frame maps to 100,000 entries. Codec/runtime admission, playable mux, audio/visual/caption sync, cancellation/readback, rights, and canonical project-revision integration remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Public multitrack `Plan` construction now also enforces the existing 1..8 GiB declared-output budget used by `Graph`; direct construction rejects zero and above-limit budgets. `MultitrackRenderPlannerTest` passes 15/15. This validates plan metadata only; codec/runtime admission, playable mux, synchronization, cancellation/readback, rights and canonical project-revision integration remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Public Plan construction now requires the exact multitrack capability ID, positive duration within 24 hours, header fields matching duration/output budget, SHA-256 matching the manifest, and manifest frame rows matching the frame map. - **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `MultitrackRenderPlannerTest` now checks the lower source interval boundary: a frame timestamp before a trimmed clip’s exact `sourceIn` is rejected, complementing the upper-bound/source-to-timeline mapping checks. **`media.dev.quality-comparison-and-optimization`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `modules/production/rendering`, `audio-video-observability`, `libs/audio-video-ui`
- **What:** Measure quality from real outputs and apply bounded reversible optimization.

- **Test/exit:** Reference metrics/golden compare, invalid bounds, multiple attempts, deterministic report, undo.

Still OPEN: comparison consumes caller-supplied decoded RGB frames and has no admitted real decoder/reference host or durable reversible project-revision wiring. 2026-11-06 increment: SSIM now uses single-pass population moments instead of allocating full-image luma arrays; metric loops read the immutable private RGB snapshots while the public frame accessor stays defensive. Still OPEN: no admitted real decoder/reference host or durable reversible project-revision wiring.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Public `Report` and `Run` result records now bound retained revisions/comparisons from `MAX_ATTEMPTS` before copying and stop actual iteration at the limit. Hostile-list, underreported-iterator and valid-result regressions pass in `QualityComparisonAndOptimizationTest` 19/19. Result-object invariants only; real decoder/reference quality and durable reversible project revision remain open.
- **Bounded input increment (2026-11-06):** The revision constructor now limits actual iterator consumption before copying, so an underreported frame-list size cannot bypass `MAX_FRAMES`. `QualityComparisonAndOptimizationTest` passes 13/13, including the over-limit traversal regression. Real decoder/reference qualification and durable reversible project-revision wiring remain open.
- **Memory accounting increment (2026-11-06):** Aggregate byte accounting now reads the immutable nested RGB snapshot length directly instead of cloning each frame payload merely to count its bytes. The public defensive-copy accessor is unchanged. `QualityComparisonAndOptimizationTest` passed 13/13; real decoder/reference qualification and durable reversible project-revision wiring remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Canonical quality-report hashing now length-prefixes caller-controlled operation/decision strings and revision identity components, with an explicit `quality-comparison-v2` format marker. Real decoder/reference-host qualification and durable reversible project-revision wiring remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** QualityComparisonAndOptimization.RevisionId now rejects surrounding whitespace on tenant, principal, artifact, and version identities instead of silently trimming exact immutable revision refs. **`media.dev.delivery-export-destination-ack`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `modules/production/delivery`, `launcher`, `providers/aws-postgresql`
- **What:** Implement download/export/publication with exact destination acknowledgment.
- **How:** Produce bounded immutable artifact; sign scoped transfer; distinguish queued/sent/destination confirmed; never infer from transport 201.
- **Test/exit:** Wrong destination, lost ACK, token revoke, duplicate export, checksum and resume.
- **Progress:** Delivery results return the exact artifact ID and immutable version reference; a lost-ACK resume regression preserves that tuple. V031 persists operation snapshots, and additive V032 adds an exact-scope PostgreSQL claim with DB-time expiry and a monotonic fence. Claim acquisition compares stored status and acknowledged offset; stores reject illegal state regressions and decreasing acknowledged offsets, and unfenced state writes cannot replace an active claim. `MediaDeliveryEngine` crosses the destination boundary only through `FencedDestinationPort` while holding a durable claim; legacy destinations and process-local stores fail closed. `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` must be durably written before each external call, and a false barrier update leaves the operation queued without calling the destination. A restart regression covers recovery when a crash leaves the operation `QUEUED` before that pre-dispatch barrier. The delivery module passes 22/22; PostgreSQL claim/store tests pass 2/2; migration-lineage and IP06 selectors pass 18/18. The PostgreSQL adapter is not wired into the default runtime, no real destination currently enforces stale fences, and a destination call longer than the claim lease still depends on that enforcement to prevent overlapping external effects. Signed authorization and real destination reconciliation remain open; no publication or destination ACK is claimed. 2026-11-06 increment: wrong chunk digest leaves the operation durably OUTCOME_UNKNOWN at acknowledged offset 0 with no receipt reference; exact destination reconciliation confirms it without resending the accepted chunk. MediaDeliveryEngineTest PASS 23/23. This is local fixture evidence only. Signed transfer authorization, real destination/receipt, production wiring, and destination-side stale-fence enforcement remain open. 2026-11-06 increment: Added null-status rejection for durable delivery operation snapshots and returned delivery results; malformed rows/results now fail at construction before transition handling. MediaDeliveryEngineTest PASS 24/24. Local contract hardening only; signed transfer authorization, real destination/receipt reconciliation, production wiring, and destination-side stale-fence enforcement remain open. 2026-11-06 increment: PostgreSQL claim acquisition and renewal now round positive fractional milliseconds up instead of truncating; the submillisecond Testcontainers regression verifies the lease stays live and returned expiry exactly equals persisted `timestamptz`. `PostgresqlMediaDeliveryOperationStoreTest` PASS 3/3. Destination-side stale-fence enforcement, signed authorization, real receipt reconciliation, and production wiring remain open.

**`media.dev.offline-reconnect`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `modules/production/delivery`, `libs/audio-video-client`, `libs/audio-video-ui`
- **What:** Support private local edits/capture with safe reconnect and conflict reconciliation.
- **How:** Bound encrypted offline queue per tenant/project, renew rights before push, compare version and idempotency; never replay after revoke.
- **Test/exit:** Offline dirty state, conflict resolution, stale authorization, loss/retry and no duplicate effect.
- **Progress:** Replay now atomically fences each edit as `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` before external dispatch, then CASes to final state only on a valid receipt/conflict. Idempotency metadata is authenticated with AES-GCM; old stores remain source-compatible and return explicit `OFFLINE_STORE_CAS_UNAVAILABLE` without authority reads or dispatch. Store exceptions during CAS are treated as indeterminate: pre-dispatch failure prevents the effect; post-dispatch failure preserves the durable unknown fence and prevents replay. Added the exported `IndexedDbOfflineEditStore` with tenant/project scoping, unique scoped idempotency, transaction-backed entry bounds/CAS, defensive ciphertext copies, sanitized errors and retry after failed/blocked opens. Queue, reconnect and adapter lifecycle tests pass 26/26; client typecheck and installed ESM/CJS/types consumers pass for the new subpath. **2026-11-06 increment:** a real Chromium selector uses intercepted HTTPS routes to prove two pages share IndexedDB, cross-page concurrent CAS has exactly one winner, and close/reopen plus page reload preserve the exact scoped record and nonce/ciphertext; `test:browser:offline-store` passes. A J-23 browser workflow now persists an AES-GCM edit in IndexedDB, reloads the app frame while a host page retains and resupplies the same non-extractable `CryptoKey`, confirms stale-version conflict before authorization or dispatch, then explicitly rebases with a new idempotency key and performs fresh authorization plus a version-matched local fixture APPLIED effect exactly once. The key stays outside the edit store. This is synthetic client/store recovery only; it does not prove production key custody, real rights authority, canonical project integration or qualified effects. Focused offline tests pass 26/26, typecheck and development-register checks pass. The canonical project-edit operation and authenticated runtime/UI binding remain open. 2026-11-06 increment: Unknown-outcome discard now conditionally removes the exact persisted ciphertext/state/version record; IndexedDB performs read-and-delete inside one readwrite transaction. Stores without conditional removal fail with STORE_CAS_UNAVAILABLE; a changed row is preserved with STORE_RECORD_CHANGED. OfflineEditQueue tests PASS 14/14 and client typecheck PASS. Still OPEN: canonical project-edit integration, production key/authority custody, and durable canonical restart/effect evidence. 2026-11-06 increment: An `APPLIED` response with a non-string receipt is treated as `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`; after queue recreation, the record stays fenced and no second apply occurs. Focused offline selector PASS 12/12; client typecheck PASS. Production key/authority and canonical integration remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Reconnect now refreshes authorization after the durable pre-dispatch fence, closing the window where rights could be revoked during the storage CAS. A revocation injected during that write transitions the edit to `REVOKED` with zero apply calls; the offline queue, reconnect and IndexedDB selectors pass 30/30, client typecheck passes, and the real Chromium J-23 selector passes with the added post-fence authorization read. If the second read is unknown, replay returns to `PENDING` only after a confirmed CAS; otherwise it retains `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`. Canonical project-edit integration, production key/authority custody, and durable canonical effect/readback remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Offline queue decryption now passes only typed edit fields to authorization and apply adapters; stored nonce, ciphertext, replay state and byte metadata stay inside the store. The focused `offline-reconnect.test.ts` suite passes 13/13 and client typecheck passes. The browser selector built but Chromium did not launch because macOS denied its bootstrap permission. Canonical project-edit integration, production key/authority custody and durable canonical effect/readback remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `OfflineReconnectCoordinator` now maps null, wrong-scope or unrecognized authorization dispositions to `UNKNOWN` rather than false `REVOKED`. Regression proves the edit remains `PENDING`, zero apply effects occur, and a later valid readback can recover. `offline-reconnect.test.ts` passes 14/14 and client typecheck passes. Canonical project-edit integration, production key/rights authority custody and durable effect/readback remain open.

Task remains OPEN.

- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Added deterministic source-time progress to the player reducer: it accepts only finite integer, nondecreasing positions inside the active scene while playing, then pauses at the scene boundary and asks the viewer to choose or replay without selecting a branch. `pnpm test:interactive-demo-player-state` passes 3/3 on Node v26.7.0; equivalent UI regressions were added to the existing Vitest suite. This proves reducer behavior only. The renderer still has no media element or source-time event wiring; UI Vitest, browser playback, real-output fidelity and host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** The verified player now attaches a native video source only while the host attestation is READY, positions it to the authored scene start before enabling Play, maps native play/pause/time/end/error events to the reducer, and stops at scene end without auto-branching. Playback failure is typed and sanitized. Readiness is bound to the current video element as well as its scene key, so revisiting a prior scene cannot reuse stale readiness. `pnpm test:interactive-demo-player-state` passes 5/5; UI Vitest and typecheck remain unavailable (`vitest/config` unresolved and `tsc` missing). Assumption: the existing `startMs`/`endMs` values are offsets on the authored video timeline; the manifest has no media-kind/MIME field and examples use MP4. No live-output claim or new origin permission is made.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** 2026-11-06 branch focus increment: after an active scene change, focus moves to the scene label so keyboard users retain a meaningful location when the selected branch button unmounts. A regression assertion was added from PDP-3 keyboard-hotspot intent. The targeted UI Vitest test could not start because `vitest/config` is unavailable; the independent player-state Node suite passes 5/5 and does not exercise the React focus change. Browser playback, UI test/typecheck, live-output fidelity and host qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** Interactive source-truth validation now rejects whitespace-padded curation/live evidence and bundle-verification refs instead of presenting them as exact. Browser playback, UI test/typecheck, live-output fidelity and host qualification remain open.

Player state suite remains 5/5, but the React UI test could not collect because `vitest/config` is unavailable, so the clamp regression is unverified. Browser playback and real-output fidelity remain open.

This proves local wrapper behavior only; durable outcome binding, process restart and real outage qualification remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** 2026-11-07 typed-provider-UNKNOWN increment: `MediaRuntimeJobTimeoutTest.providerReportedUnknownFinalityStaysUnknownWithoutFallbackOrResult` verifies one selected-provider dispatch, `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`, no result/caption, and zero fallback dispatch for a typed UNKNOWN response. The focused FULL source-composite selector passes 1/1. Fixture provider/store only; real outage behavior, durable restart reconciliation, admitted fallback, and provider qualification remain open.
- **Predecessors:** `media.dev.canonical-job-reconciliation`, `media.dev.service-observability`

Export throws typed `SourceRightsDeniedException(UNKNOWN)` after checking both exact source versions. Focused FULL source-composite selector passes 2/2. This remains module-local rights-boundary evidence; no encoded export, real viewer fidelity or consumer receipt is established.

Focused selector passes 3/3. This proves local output-copy immutability only; canonical render/output joining, destination authority/receipt, 360 conventions, and viewer fidelity remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** The registered J-28 encoded-candidate scenario corrupts the GLB declared length, recomputes the digest, and confirms structural `GlbArtifact` validation rejects it. Both registered J-28 selector cases pass. Fixture only; real viewer/360 fidelity, destination authority/receipt, canonical output lineage, and runtime integration remain open.

Focused spatial class passes 4/4. Local fixture only; no encoded delivery, viewer/360 fidelity, destination authority or receipt is established.

**`media.dev.outcome-live-session-recovery`** · OPEN
- **Where:** `integration-tests`, `modules/integration-tests`, `libs/audio-video-ui`
- **What:** J-29 — Real media loss/reconnect/consent revoke and bounded stream recovery.
- **How:** Drive the authored scenario via a source-free client or real hosted UI using existing physical surfaces; bind immutable source/tenant/rights/job and exact observable effect; inject denial, cancellation, ambiguity and restart.
- **Test/exit:** Reproducible genuine terminal result and fault suite; fixture-only simulation never counts.
- **Progress:** `GuardedCrossModalityStreamStateMachineTest` passes 2/2. After detach, current-consent denial blocks reconnect/resume and rejects stale worker leases. A live-session case also changes audio/video consent to `NOT_ADMITTED`; pending provider egress and the next frame are rejected while ACK remains 0. These are in-memory fixtures. Still OPEN: canonical durable lease/cursor/PTS binding, process restart, authenticated transport, and real host session recovery.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** `MediaRuntimeUnknownStreamPrefixScenarioIT` passes 1/1: after frame 0 is acknowledged, frame 1 times out with `UNKNOWN`, the acknowledged cursor stays at 0, in-flight sequence remains 1, cancellation is signaled, and retry is blocked without a third provider call. Adjacent focused selectors also pass for consent-revoked reconnect, timeout, PostgreSQL lease expiry, restart readback, and bounded audio/video stream state. These use synthetic authorities/providers; canonical source artifact/version/PTS semantics, authenticated transport and real host/provider recovery remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** J-29 keeps the same sequence at `FENCE_AND_HOLD/UNRESOLVED_FRAME_EFFECT` when conflicting `REJECTED` evidence is also supplied; both observations remain visible and `dispatchAllowed=false`. The stream-recovery definition selector passes 7/7. Definition fixture only; durable source cursor/PTS, authenticated transport, restart and real host/provider recovery remain open.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-07):** 2026-11-07 ambiguous stream-effect increment: throwing fixture sinks yield `ProviderOutcomeUnknown`; audio reconnect and video lease claim/egress replay remain fenced, acknowledgments stay at zero, and the two initial sink calls are not repeated. `GuardedCrossModalityStreamStateMachineTest` passes 3/3. In-memory only; canonical durable reconciliation, authenticated transport, source version/PTS and real consent/provider authority remain open.
- **Predecessors:** `media.dev.stream-session-authority`

**`media.dev.outcome-eligible-provider-catalog`** · OPEN

- **What:** J-30 — Real qualified model/voice/host readiness with unavailable cases.
- **How:** Drive the authored scenario via a source-free client or real hosted UI using existing physical surfaces; bind immutable source/tenant/rights/job and exact observable effect; inject denial, cancellation, ambiguity and restart.
- **Test/exit:** Reproducible genuine terminal result and fault suite; fixture-only simulation never counts.
- **Progress:** Source review found no admitted composite catalog seam: current model asset readiness, STT/TTS status, Vision capability admission and host health are separate facts and cannot be combined as qualification. PDP-0 requires distinct profile/capability/provider/license/qualification/runtime dimensions in a bounded exact-target observation with provenance, UNKNOWN for missing/stale evidence, and no future-availability promise. PDP-3 leaves permission names, freshness windows, response schema, authority and reason codes pending owner review; the scoped-query operation is definition-grade only and runtime `NOT_ADMITTED`/dispatch `NEVER`. J-30 has no exact scenario seed. Owner-semantics handoff: define these response/freshness/authority contracts before an aggregate API/test is added; no eligibility inference or provider selection is implemented.
- **Additional progress (2026-11-06):** The census now includes one local valid-but-unadmitted Vision selector that rejects before provider effects. It does not build the missing composite readiness catalog or resolve the exact J-30 seed, freshness or authority semantics. No eligibility inference or provider selection was added; the owner-semantic handoff remains and J-30 stays OPEN.

Focused TTS selector PASS; development work-register PASS 3/3. This is one local boundary, not a composite catalog. Exact-target response/freshness/authority/reason semantics and scenario seed remain owner-review pending; qualified model/voice/detector and host evidence remain absent.

## Demo/business outcome policy

Re-use the PDP3 authored Explorer semantic scenario and narrated source data, but never call a fixture recording actual product behavior. First run the real J01 → J02 → J20 → J03 journey with legal source speech, durable job restart, transcript-correction and exact caption revision; then record the same semantic beats against the real host. Follow with each of J04–J30 as its required physical engines land. For every journey, an actual correct positive effect, denied/UNKNOWN path, restart/finality and source-free consumer check are mandatory.

## Operationally independent execution

- **Parallel owners:** foundations (rights/store), modalities (STT/TTS/Vision/native), interfaces (SDK/HTTP/gRPC/Tools), capture, creative subengines, hygiene classification. Serialize edits to shared public contracts and writer semantics; no parallel duplicate implementations.
- **First tests after safety commit:** `./gradlew :services:media:adapters:agent-tools-media:test :services:media:adapters:ledger:test` in the supported repository source-composite profile; run full affected integration tests after focused suites. If single-host assumptions cannot be proven on a target filesystem, keep delegated effects disabled.
- **Source-free TS checks:** `pnpm --filter @audio-video/types check`, `pnpm --filter @audio-video/client test:package`, `pnpm --filter @audio-video/ui check`, followed by real browser/client route and journey tests.
- **Other relevant tests:** native Rust, provider contract and fault suites, Testcontainers PostgreSQL/S3/OCI worker, 1/16/64 resource budgets, a11y/CSP and actual encoded media verification.
- **Owner-only gates do not block unrelated work:** real vendor rights, voice consent, DI installed endpoint and production host access limit only their exact positive integration/qualified mode; they never justify faking success or stopping code/docs/security tests.

## Clean-repository finish

After each task's consumers are updated, remove old internal wrappers, test-only/orphan directories, and dated redundant documents. Keep PDP semantic IDs, externally published `audio-video` ABI/protos/routes and DB migration versions until a tested compatibility migration. The current root `README.md`, `AGENTS.md` and docs navigation must select these two development documents, not historical run context. Keep an exception registry for named immutable source artifacts whose version/date is semantically required.

## Claims boundary

This is a current-source, owner-approved implementation plan. It does not assert fresh Gradle, pnpm, JUnit, Rust, container, browser or model-inference results; it does not provide external credentials, license rights or production admission. A task exits only on its requested tests and real contract/behavior. The repo does not yet meet whole-product development completeness.
